# The Wastes cleanup — task register

**Single source of truth for work status and next action.** Updated 2026-11-02. The Wastes campaign is complete through S3E18; the eleven-arc reader edition is available inside the **private repository**, not on a public website.

The checkbox beside a task is its only completion flag. **[x]** means the defined deliverable was checked at its stated scope, *not* that the whole phase, every linked page or original audio is verified. **[ ]** means open, including deliberately deferred or conditional work. Stable `WCL-###` IDs must **never be reused or renumbered**; refer to a task as [WCL-029](#wcl-029) in any other document. The bare heading keeps its link stable even if the title changes.

**Current next action:** [WCL-077](#wcl-077) — audit family, character and antagonist pages for unsupported identities of the apparent second Nash and the unnamed person in his final warning; broader WCL-032 remains open. **Recently completed:** [WCL-082](#wcl-082) (E039 potion transfer verified; no witnessed payoff, narrative left intact) and parent [WCL-040](#wcl-040) (all five chronology/source-boundary subtasks accepted); [WCL-078](#wcl-078) (archive/story/knowledge clocks separated, stale E081/E090 cutoffs corrected); [WCL-075](#wcl-075) and [WCL-076](#wcl-076) (Worldbreaker banishment and separately evidenced Earth restoration); [WCL-074](#wcl-074) (Elhandar fall, rescue and dispersed-survivor history); [WCL-095](#wcl-095) and parent [WCL-036](#wcl-036) (current review and claim routing completed); [WCL-094](#wcl-094) (active source-review routing and stale-pointer cleanup); [WCL-100](#wcl-100) and parent [WCL-035](#wcl-035) (first v3 source-bounded relationship and custody graph); [WCL-200](#wcl-200) (six added people records, Stanley alias and focused compatibility tests); [WCL-099](#wcl-099) (entity graph inventory and migration plan); [WCL-098](#wcl-098) (Or'Nath name regression) and parent [WCL-034](#wcl-034) (canonical-name/alias regression); [WCL-097](#wcl-097) (Khaervilon/Kervalon map name and retained full-body alias), [WCL-096](#wcl-096) (The Principal spelling), [WCL-197](#wcl-197) (legacy placeholder/backlink cleanup), [WCL-199](#wcl-199) (historical image links), [WCL-198](#wcl-198) (episode source links), [WCL-195](#wcl-195) (canonical/provisional primary names), and [WCL-196](#wcl-196) (race origin histories). Parent [WCL-034](#wcl-034) is now closed at the scoped static naming/navigation level; native-browser checks remain separately open under WCL-103. Live world census: **149 unique paths** (133 of 134 original paths retained plus 16 additions); acceptance matrix: **332 maintained paths**. WCL-103 native-browser/external-bookmark checks remain deferred. The repository is private; no public deployment is claimed.

**How to maintain this register:** Add a task when an independently verifiable work item is discovered; choose its stable next ID and a precise outcome. Check it off only after reading back the actual changed files and recording relevant evidence, validation and limits in that **same task's entry**. An interruption leaves it unchecked with a short recovery note. Under [D-082](DECISIONS.md), group compatible tasks and related files into the largest manageable **adaptive cohort**, saving per-file evidence incrementally and updating this register once per coherent batch where possible. Check off each WCL task individually only when all selected required outputs pass; an unfinished subset stays unchecked. Split oversized or separately testable material work into new IDs; do not mark a whole group done because a sample passed. Cite an existing owner decision for substantive work. Do **not** append the same batch recap to the manifest, changelog and progress file. Existing source dossiers, decision records, review issue keys and one-time original archives remain independent evidence.

### WCL-045

- [x] **Separate Nash's historical cutoffs from campaign-end status.** Read-only cross-check completed 2026-10-31 at `main` HEAD `076e1d5`; parent [WCL-029](#wcl-029). Evidence: [Nash biography](../world/characters/nash-maggard.md), [heart history](../world/technology/nash-dragon-heart.md), played [S3E03](../episodes/S3E03.md), [S3E10](../episodes/S3E10.md), [S3E11](../episodes/S3E11.md), [S3E13](../episodes/S3E13.md), [S3E14](../episodes/S3E14.md), [S3E18](../episodes/S3E18.md) dossiers; no original audio listened.

  - **Physical and custody sequence:** The engineered metal heart remained implanted through S3E12 after S2E12 heart/eyestalk survival and S3E01–02 regenerated bodily restoration; Brett/Asmodeus physically removed it in S3E13 and made Nash a beating heart from Nash's *own* flesh. Brett is its **last witnessed holder**, not a proven original manufacturer. Nash successfully used *Firebolt* and innate floating afterward. The separate natural black-dragon heart recovered for Ruby is **not** his engine.
  - **Transformation and finale:** The S3E13 statement “not yet tested” is true **at that cutoff only**; a partial, incomplete dragon attempt was played in S3E14 and full adult-dragon restoration was not shown by the finale. In S3E18 Earth/Moon ordinary spellcasting was suppressed but floating persisted. Council of Faces forcibly made Nash a chrome Principal successor; Nash independently resisted its murder order, evacuated the allies, detonated the moon facility, and **actually died** as his chrome body melted. Stanley later used *Wish* to produce *True Resurrection* away from Earth; Nash returned to familiar flesh **without equipment**, reunited with Angelashi and four children and stayed on Planet Nash. No witnessed reimplantation of the metal engine, anatomical examination of his resurrected heart or successful new dragon transformation. An apparent second Nash in the final cupboard scene remains unidentified.
  - **Origin provenance:** S3E03's alleged childhood, paternal seed and 78-body origins are **parasite-mediated dream claims**; S3E10's operating slab is physically observed but Nash's suggested implantation location is inference; S3E11's test-tube/weapon/adjacency account is the Principal's testimony; S3E12's alleged loan comes from Asmodeus; S3E18's Council-of-Faces ancestry account is also interested testimony. None proves biological paternity, exact construction or full timelines. Early dragon-mother/Clench accounts likewise remain attributed, not independent facts.
  - **Cutoff classification:** S2E08/S2E11/S2E12, S3E01/S3E02 and the pre-removal S3E03–12 possession, powers and family locations are historical snapshots, **not live present state**; S3E13 “not tested,” S3E17 “Ruby unactivated” and older heart-magic durability theories are superseded/qualified by later played evidence. The story-first `Development by arc` currently ends after the old Arc Five and does not itself narrate the later campaign, while latest-first research sections above and below it contain those events. The factual page largely distinguishes cutoffs already; the unresolved deficiency is **reader presentation and coverage**, not license to replace uncertain testimony with fact. WCL-046 determines treatment; WCL-030 remains a separate edit gate.

### WCL-056

- [x] **Assess Harlequinari as a people/culture subject.** Completed **2026-10-31**, dossier and targeted dual-ASR review at `main` HEAD `d13b9c5`, with explicit consideration of the owner's **Council of Clowns–Harlequinari hypothesis**. **Parent:** [WCL-012](#wcl-012). **Disposition:** distinct people/culture coverage is supported, but **the Council's complete species membership is not proved**. Author a narrowly qualified reader-first culture page under follow-up [WCL-123](#wcl-123), linking the Council as an affiliated institution, not equating a faction's entire membership with a people.

  - **Evidence:** [E113](../episodes/E113.md) explicitly shows Blood Skunk identifying himself as Harlequinari to ferryman Hesterbrook (about **01:04**), being treated as an exile (**01:10–01:11**), and telling the Council of Clowns that he had **left the Harlequinari long ago and no longer served them** (about **02:23**). Bobo and the council speak of a wider **Harlequinari world**, and mention **many Harlequinari** who could attend a proposed humor workshop (about **02:17**), so an in-world cultural community and connection with the Council are evidenced. The potentially misleading line **“a Harlequinari on your council”** (about **02:22**) is directed **to Hope about Blood Skunk among her companions**; it is not identification of all thirteen official Council members. Both [Parakeet](../sources/The%20Wastes%20S1E113.%20I%20Need%20To%20Smell%20Your%20Hand.parakeet.processed.srt) and [Qwen-labelled](../sources/The%20Wastes%20S1E113.%20I%20Need%20To%20Smell%20Your%20Hand.qwen-q8.canary-ctc.nemotron.srt) SRT windows were read and compared for those lines. [E088](../episodes/E088.md) establishes the Council's valued cruel trickery through Kidigree's account; [E103](../episodes/E103.md) features a **Blood Skunk-specific** `Critical Fortunes` mechanic, not a verified universal trait. The [September bonus](../episodes/BONUS-2020-09.md) has an ASR-disputed identity clause that cannot nullify E113's later direct self-identification. The Council itself appears in Pandemonium and is distinct from the broader population; no population census, uniform physiology, inherited rules, place of birth or racial status of each councilor was provided. No original audio audition, complete SRT concordance or independent designer rules sheet; spellings/attributions remain provisional.

  - **Result:** The race-versus-faction distinction is better supported than the original tentative Races-index note suggested. [WCL-123](#wcl-123) owns the source-footnoted new article and careful navigation/cross-link repairs. The audit itself makes no world-article changes; completed only after this source comparison. **Next dependent work:** [WCL-123](#wcl-123), then [WCL-057](#wcl-057).

### WCL-061

- [x] **Assess pixies as a people/culture subject.** Completed **2026-10-31** from bounded E031–E054, E056, E110 and S2E04 episode dossiers and existing character/creature references at `main` HEAD `8517fc2`. **Parent:** [WCL-012](#wcl-012). **Disposition:** enough evidence exists for a **cautiously bounded Pixies people profile** about persons, kinship, captivity and differing outcomes, but **not** for an asserted common civilization, Feywild homeland, law, religion, or innate cultural values. Its separately verifiable article and cross-links are assigned to [WCL-128](#wcl-128).

  - **Evidence and limits:** [E031](../episodes/E031.md) directly shows winged, speaking **P0D**, labeled with an aquarium number, asking not to be returned to Farmer Dewey; she **reports** numbered captive pixies crushed for Pixie Blush and wishes the others freed. The underground farm is **not directly inspected or completely liberated** in these scenes, and plans do not imply action; [BONUS-2019-03](../episodes/BONUS-2019-03.md) separately identifies a GM-prepared **unchosen pixie-rescue route**. [E049](../episodes/E049.md) shows **Monsoon's Order 26** forcibly transforming P0D against her will, and [E050](../episodes/E050.md) directly shows **Diontalus killing her**; her later [E056](../episodes/E056.md) spirit encounter is not physical survival. Other enlarged pixies attack at [E051](../episodes/E051.md)–[E052](../episodes/E052.md), and **some revert and disperse** in [E054](../episodes/E054.md), not proof of the fates of all captive pixies, or a hereditary species form; existing [Pixie Hulks](../world/creatures/pixie-hulks.md) is the **monstrous forced-transformation** article. [S2E04](../episodes/S2E04.md) and [E110](../episodes/E110.md) directly observe a **different, alternate Pod** living at Tower Nash with pixie partner **Roger**, two children and another expected; she stays with her family. This demonstrates individual pixie kinship beyond the farm, not resurrection of original P0D, universal household custom or an independently surveyed community. ### WCL-196

- [x] **Add campaign-backed origin histories to the Wastes peoples and cultures profiles.** **Related parent:** [WCL-012](#wcl-012); source-boundary work [WCL-040](#wcl-040). **Owner policy:** [D-084](DECISIONS.md). **Deliverable:** Revisit the eight maintained [Wastes people profiles](../world/RACES.md) and add meaningful **Origins / Ancient history** narrative where the campaign actually supplies origins, ancestral movements, deliberate creation/breeding, divine accounts, shared migrations or competing explanations. **Priority example: Lizardfolk** — explain the revealed **bioengineered/experimentally bred Wastes lineage** and its significance, while cross-checking [E078](../episodes/E078.md) (CHIDS's claim that *preexisting otherworldly lizardfolk* were sampled/bred rather than the entire species invented), [S3E06](../episodes/S3E06.md) (hostile Paladin slave-origin testimony), [S3E09](../episodes/S3E09.md) (Groxl's claimed laboratory upbringing) and any corroborating episodes or creator/GM explanations. Separate **established events, attributed in-world history, uncertain universals and unresolved origins**; a lineage engineered in the Wastes is not automatically all lizardfolk in every world. Carry the same source-specific discipline across Tieflings, Pixies, Harlequinari, Elves, Orcs, Dwarves and Illithids, omitting unsupported origins instead of filling gaps with generic D&D lore. Keep people distinct from factions/creatures and do not rewrite individual biographies as species facts. Add episode-linked footnotes, reader-first explanations, cross-links from `world/RACES.md`, and appropriate final-status/context where relevant. Validate each changed profile's links, footnotes, headings and lint; update its existing census row after measured changes. Split into race-specific indexed subtasks if the source research becomes too large; do not claim the E078/S3E06/S3E09 accounts are all independently confirmed by the currently unread original audio. **Acceptance:** every maintained people profile has been evaluated for origin coverage, each supported history is written, and any remaining claims/unknowns are explicitly attributed. No primary-source audio verification, commit, push or publication implied.

  - **2026-11-02 WCL-196 completed at its source-limited acceptance scope (D-084):** Reviewed **all eight** maintained peoples/cultures profiles, their existing episode-based footnotes and actual origin coverage, distinguishing regional/cultural histories from whole-species genesis. **Lizardfolk** received a new reader-first **Origins and the engineered lineages** section: the CHIDS council's E078 account of taking genes from *pre-existing otherworldly lizardfolk* and breeding anti-dragon soldiers, the S3E06 Paladin's **hostile slave-creation doctrine**, and Groxl's S3E09 **self-attributed laboratory upbringing, attempted founding of Razak Val and alleged infertility**. Their conflicts remain distinct, no claim of universal lizardfolk manufacture or Vorel's sterility is asserted, and Vorel's rejection of slavery/revenge is shown in the story. Three new episode-linked footnotes separate evidence tiers and audio limits. **Tieflings** gained a named ancient-kingdom origins section distinguishing witnessed buried T'ietkallan remains from Nobieri's interested Akmenos-lineage and old-pact history (E047–E048). **Elves** gained an Elhandari ancient-mission section and a less repetitive sanctuary section: E115 elder testimony about pre-Scorching restoration and soul beliefs is not mistaken for the origin or universal spirituality of every elf. Existing notes/footnotes support both. **Dwarves:** the ancient skeletal settlement, Queen Amelda/Quizquaz and contested Moag Ferhain identification are already reader-first, with no supported shared biological origin. **Harlequinari:** E113 proves Blood Skunk's identification, council contact and reported wider world, not the species' first creation or that every clown belongs to it. **Orcs:** documented Koromukishi institution, Liberator dissent and distinct mercenaries, not a universal ancestor story. **Illithids:** CHIDS hostile cohort, uncertain Void Realm and independent Fax appearances, not a demonstrated mind-flayer biological origin. **Pixies:** P0D's testimony of captive extraction and separately observed alternative Pod household, not universal pixie ancestry or a witnessed original homeland. Unsupported origins were deliberately **not fabricated**. `world/RACES.md` now introduces the three expanded histories directly from its linked people rows; current status remains the last narrative section in each edited people article.

  - **Consumer and documentation verification:** Updated [ENTITY_SCHEMA.md](../world/ENTITY_SCHEMA.md#wcl-200--first-people-record-expansion-2026-11-02), [world/README.md](../world/README.md), [world/RACES.md](../world/RACES.md), lifecycle [manifest](WASTES_CLEANUP_MANIFEST.md) and [acceptance matrix](WASTES_FULL_FILE_ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md) to distinguish the now-indexed eight people from still incomplete wider world/entity graph. Scoped Markdownlint **5/5 changed supporting Markdown pages** (schema, Races, world README, manifest, acceptance matrix) passed with zero violations. Offline renderer checked schema **46/46**, world README **86/86**, Races **33/33**, acceptance matrix **9/9**, manifest **100/127** links — **0 broken among 274 checked**, with **27 manifest links beyond preview cap** unrendered. A separately bounded **read-only static reference diagnostic** scanned **365/367 permitted world + episode + narrative Markdown files and 8,012 parsed links** with **zero missing-target or fragment findings** within supported syntax; the two capability-restricted world pages were excluded, and **347 name-form, 42 missing-status and three finale heuristics** remain independent editorial review candidates rather than test failures.
